INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE (MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID) WITH MEA1 AS ( SELECT U.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE , NVL(M.ATTRIBUTE5, U.COLUMN_NAME) AS RESULT_MEASURE , U.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP , CAST(U.SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2)) AS SORT_ORDER_NO , U.USER_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE M ON M.PROGRAM_ID = U.PROGRAM_ID AND M.SHEET_NAME = U.SHEET_NAME AND M.COLUMN_NAME = U.COLUMN_NAME AND M.VALID_YN = 'Y' WHERE U.PROGRAM_ID = :B3 AND U.SHEET_NAME = :B2 AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M' AND U.USER_ID = :B1 ), MEA2 AS ( SELECT S.MEASURE, S.MEASURE AS RESULT_MEASURE, 'CUST_FCST' AS MEASURE_GROUP , CAST(A.SORT_ORDER_NO + S.SORT_NO AS NUMBER(10,2)) AS SORT_ORDER_NO, A.USER_ID FROM (SELECT U.USER_ID, U.SORT_ORDER_NO FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U WHERE U.PROGRAM_ID = :B3 AND U.SHEET_NAME = :B2 AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M' AND U.USER_ID = :B1 AND U.COLUMN_NAME = 'CUST_FCST') A, (SELECT MEASURE, MIN(SORT_NO) AS SORT_NO FROM (SELECT 'CUST_FCST_EDI' AS MEASURE, 0.11 AS SORT_NO FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Backorder' , 0.12 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Commercial_Agreement' , 0.13 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Firm_Temp' , 0.14 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Immidiate' , 0.15 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Manumacturing_Material_Temp' , 0.16 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Material_Temp' , 0.17 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Planning_Forecast' , 0.18 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT C.MEASURE, 0.10 FROM SCM_MGR.DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST C WHERE C.MEASURE_GROUP = 'CUST_FCST' AND NVL(C.MEASURE, '/') <> '/' AND C.VALID_YN = 'Y') GROUP BY MEASURE) S WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM MEA1 X WHERE X.MEASURE = S.MEASURE) ), MEA3 AS ( SELECT M.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE, M.COLUMN_NAME AS RESULT_MEASURE, M.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP , CAST(M.SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2)) AS SORT_ORDER_NO, 'DM_ADMIN' AS USER_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE M WHERE M.PROGRAM_ID = :B3 AND M.SHEET_NAME = :B2 AND M.COLUMN_NAME IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'FCST_GAP', 'RTF_SYS_RP', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW', 'SCM_FCST', 'MKT_FCST') AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM MEA1 X WHERE X.MEASURE = M.COLUMN_NAME) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM MEA2 X WHERE X.MEASURE = M.COLUMN_NAME) ) SELECT MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID FROM MEA1 UNION ALL SELECT MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID FROM MEA2 UNION ALL SELECT MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID FROM MEA3

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY) SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE, MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID WHERE (:B3 LIKE '81%' OR :B3 = '82') AND A.DIVISION_ID = :B3 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE UNION ALL SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE, MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID WHERE (:B3 IS NULL OR (:B3 NOT LIKE '81%' AND :B3 <> '82')) AND A.DIVISION_ID = :B3 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE

END OF STMT

A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.VERSION_NO , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE, MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_C A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION V ON V.VERSION_NO = A.VERSION_NO WHERE (:B5 LIKE '81%' OR :B5 = '82') AND A.DIVISION_ID = :B5 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B4 AND :B3 AND A.CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD_DATE BETWEEN TO_DATE(CASE WHEN :B2 = '00000000' THEN '19000101' ELSE :B2 END, 'YYYYMMDD') AND TO_DATE(CASE WHEN :B1 = '99999999' THEN '99991231' ELSE :B1 END, 'YYYYMMDD') GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.VERSION_NO UNION ALL SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.VERSION_NO , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE, MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN_C A INNER JOIN PARSING IN CURSOR #140563311625160 len=9412 dep=1 uid=163 oct=3 lid=163 tim=5375784972649 hv=2979442129 ad='18667f5e8' sqlid='24ktttystdbfj'

WITH INTOG AS (
    SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE
    FROM  (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS
                , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE
           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
           CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                               FROM   DUAL
                               CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT
           WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
           AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
           AND (A.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
             OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
           AND    A.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
           AND    A.SEGMENT3 = '40'
          ) B
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                        FROM   DUAL
                        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM
    WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
), ADM0 AS (
    SELECT A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE
         , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.SEGMENT4) THEN 'Y'
                WHEN EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1
                    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL T
                    WHERE  T.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                    AND    T.VALID_YN = 'Y'
                    AND   (T.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
                           OR T.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
                    AND    T.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT3 = '40'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT4 = A.SEGMENT4
                    AND    REPLACE(T.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
               ) THEN 'T'
                ELSE 'N' END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
         , CASE WHEN A.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS TODAY_LIMIT_YN
         , A.ATTRIBUTE1 AS TODAY_DAY_LIST
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
    WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
    AND    A.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
    AND    A.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
    GROUP BY A.SEGMENT4, A.ATTRIBUTE1
), ADM AS (
    SELECT A.MEASURE, A.TODAY_LIMIT_YN, A.TODAY_DAY_LIST
         , CASE WHEN A.E4 = 'Y'
                 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE)
                 AND NOT EXISTS (
                     SELECT 1
                     FROM   INTOG B
                     WHERE  B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
                     AND    NOT EXISTS (
                              SELECT 1
                              FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L2
                              WHERE  L2.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                              AND    L2.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT3    = '40'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT4    = B.MEASURE
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT1    = B.LIMIT_MEASURE
                              AND    REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                              AND    INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                           ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || 
                        AND    L2.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')))
                THEN 'N' ELSE A.E4 END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
    FROM (
        SELECT A.*
             , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1
                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                   CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(L.ATTRIBUTE4, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                                       FROM   DUAL
                                       CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ','), 0) + 1) LM
                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '40'
                   AND    REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                   ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0)
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE2, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE2 <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE3, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND    TRIM(LM.TOKEN) = A.MEASURE
                   AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L3
                                      WHERE  L3.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                      AND    L3.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT3    = '40'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT1    = TRIM(LM.TOKEN)))
                    THEN 'N' ELSE A.E3 END AS E4
        FROM (
            SELECT A.*
                 , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req'
                        THEN CASE WHEN '40' IN ('40', '82')
                                   AND NOT ( ( 'ADMIN' IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner')
                                               OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                          WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                          AND   G.USER_GR_ID IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y') )
                                             AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req') )
                                  THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                        ELSE A.E2 END AS E3
            FROM (
                SELECT A.*
                     , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'AS_In_Req'
                            THEN CASE WHEN ('40' LIKE '81%' OR '40' = '82')
                                       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                   WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                   AND   G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Planner (W/S)' AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')
                                      THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                            ELSE A.E1 END AS E2
                FROM (
                    SELECT A.*
                         , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
                                 AND ( NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                                                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '40')
                                       OR ( '40' NOT LIKE '81%'
                               OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'SCP Planner'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') )
                                            AND NOT ( 'ADMIN' = 'DM Viewer'
                                                OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Viewer'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') ) ) )
                                THEN 'Y' ELSE A.EDIT_AUTH_YN END AS E1
                    FROM ADM0 A
                ) A
            ) A
        ) A
    ) A
) SELECT MEASURE, EDIT_AUTH_YN, TODAY_LIMIT_YN, TODAY_DAY_LIST FROM ADM ORDER BY MEASURE

END OF STMT

PARSING IN CURSOR #140563313328968 len=56375 dep=1 uid=163 oct=3 lid=163 tim=5375785161794 hv=3981914282 ad='9aa66518' sqlid='97w4juvqpfc5a'

WITH INTOG AS (
    SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE
    FROM  (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS
                , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE
           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
           CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                               FROM   DUAL
                               CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT
           WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
           AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
           AND (A.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
             OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
           AND    A.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
           AND    A.SEGMENT3 = '40'
          ) B
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                        FROM   DUAL
                        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM
    WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
), ADM0 AS (
    SELECT A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE
         , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.SEGMENT4) THEN 'Y'
                WHEN EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1
                    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL T
                    WHERE  T.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                    AND    T.VALID_YN = 'Y'
                    AND   (T.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
                           OR T.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
                    AND    T.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT3 = '40'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT4 = A.SEGMENT4
                    AND    REPLACE(T.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
               ) THEN 'T'
                ELSE 'N' END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
         , CASE WHEN A.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS TODAY_LIMIT_YN
         , A.ATTRIBUTE1 AS TODAY_DAY_LIST
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
    WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
    AND    A.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
    AND    A.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
    GROUP BY A.SEGMENT4, A.ATTRIBUTE1
), ADM AS (
    SELECT A.MEASURE, A.TODAY_LIMIT_YN, A.TODAY_DAY_LIST
         , CASE WHEN A.E4 = 'Y'
                 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE)
                 AND NOT EXISTS (
                     SELECT 1
                     FROM   INTOG B
                     WHERE  B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
                     AND    NOT EXISTS (
                              SELECT 1
                              FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L2
                              WHERE  L2.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                              AND    L2.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT3    = '40'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT4    = B.MEASURE
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT1    = B.LIMIT_MEASURE
                              AND    REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                              AND    INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                           ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || 
                        AND    L2.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')))
                THEN 'N' ELSE A.E4 END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
    FROM (
        SELECT A.*
             , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1
                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                   CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(L.ATTRIBUTE4, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                                       FROM   DUAL
                                       CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ','), 0) + 1) LM
                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '40'
                   AND    REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                   ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0)
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE2, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE2 <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE3, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND    TRIM(LM.TOKEN) = A.MEASURE
                   AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L3
                                      WHERE  L3.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                      AND    L3.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT3    = '40'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT1    = TRIM(LM.TOKEN)))
                    THEN 'N' ELSE A.E3 END AS E4
        FROM (
            SELECT A.*
                 , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req'
                        THEN CASE WHEN '40' IN ('40', '82')
                                   AND NOT ( ( 'ADMIN' IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner')
                                               OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                          WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                          AND   G.USER_GR_ID IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y') )
                                             AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req') )
                                  THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                        ELSE A.E2 END AS E3
            FROM (
                SELECT A.*
                     , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'AS_In_Req'
                            THEN CASE WHEN ('40' LIKE '81%' OR '40' = '82')
                                       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                   WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                   AND   G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Planner (W/S)' AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')
                                      THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                            ELSE A.E1 END AS E2
                FROM (
                    SELECT A.*
                         , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
                                 AND ( NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                                                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '40')
                                       OR ( '40' NOT LIKE '81%'
                               OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'SCP Planner'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') )
                                            AND NOT ( 'ADMIN' = 'DM Viewer'
                                                OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'minyonglee1')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Viewer'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') ) ) )
                                THEN 'Y' ELSE A.EDIT_AUTH_YN END AS E1
                    FROM ADM0 A
                ) A
            ) A
        ) A
    ) A
)
, CAL AS (  /* L4768~4797 (주 달력 GTT 조인 WE 는 결과 무영향 — 제거) */
    SELECT D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
         , TO_CHAR(D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS BUCKET_KEY
         , 'D' AS BUCKET_TYPE
         , D.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM
         , D.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(TRIM(D.ISO_WEEK), 2, '0') AS YYYYMMWW
         , CASE WHEN WE2.WEEK_END_DATE  IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS WEEK_END_YN
         , CASE WHEN ME.MONTH_END_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS MONTH_END_YN
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS WEEK_END_DATE
               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
               WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN '20261101' AND '20270430'
               GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, ISO_WEEK) WE2
        ON  WE2.WEEK_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS MONTH_END_DATE
               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
               WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN '20261101' AND '20270430'
               GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM) ME
        ON  ME.MONTH_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
    WHERE  D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN '20261101' AND '20271017'
), PVT AS (  /* L4802~4815 */
    SELECT BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SUN' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SUN
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'MON' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS MON
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'TUE' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS TUE
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'WED' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS WED
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'THU' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS THU
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'FRI' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS FRI
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SAT' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SAT
    FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION BC
    GROUP BY BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS
)
, VD AS (  /* L4816~4822 */
    SELECT S.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(S.VMI_YN || '^' || NVL(TO_CHAR(S.ATTRIBUTE5),'')) AS VMI_STD_DIO
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S
    WHERE  S.DIVISION_ID = '82'
    AND    '40' = '82'
    GROUP BY S.SCM_SITE_ID
)
, MSP AS (
    SELECT F.DIVISION_ID, F.PROJECT_CODE, F.END_CUSTOMER, F.SCM_SITE_ID, F.ITEM_ID, F.DOM_EXP, F.FCST_TYPE, F.INCOTERMS, F.MEASURE, F.PLANT_SITE_ID, F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
         , CAL.BUCKET_TYPE, CAL.BUCKET_KEY, F.QTY AS QTY, F.MEASURE_GROUP
         , F.NICK_NAME AS NICK_NAME
         , F.OWNER AS OWNER
         , F.DEPT_ID AS DEPT_ID
         , F.ITEM_TYPE AS ITEM_TYPE
         , F.PROJECT_NAME AS PROJECT_NAME
         , F.PROJECT_OWNER AS PROJECT_OWNER
         , F.BIZ_TYPE AS BIZ_TYPE
         , F.SOLDTO_C
ITY_NAME
         , F.PROJECT_STATUS AS PROJECT_STATUS
         , F.ITEM_NAME AS ITEM_NAME
         , F.VMI_YN AS VMI_YN
         , CAST(F.CELL_PER_CAR AS NUMBER(20,10)) AS CELL_PER_CAR
         , CAST(F.CELL_PER_MODULE AS NUMBER(20,10)) AS CELL_PER_MODULE
         , CAST(F.MODULE_PER_CAR AS NUMBER(20,10)) AS MODULE_PER_CAR
         , F.CREATE_DATE AS CREATE_DATE
         , F.LAST_UPDATED_BY AS LAST_UPDATED_BY
         , F.SENDER_NAME AS SENDER_NAME
         , F.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT AS PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT
         , F.VERSION_NO AS VERSION_NO
         , F.SORT_ORDER_NO
         , NVL(F.AS_FROM_SITE, ' ') AS AS_FROM_SITE
         , F.CURRENCY AS CURRENCY
         , F.NOTATION AS NOTATION
         , CASE WHEN '40' = '82' THEN 'N'
                WHEN 'ADMIN' = 'ADMIN' THEN 'Y'
                WHEN EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM ADM MA
                    WHERE  MA.MEASURE = F.MEASURE
                    AND    MA.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'Y'
                    AND   (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'N'
                           OR (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'Y'
                               AND INSTR(
                                       REPLACE(TRIM(MA.TODAY_DAY_LIST),' ',''),
                                       CASE TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'D'))
                                           WHEN 1 THEN 'SUN' WHEN 2 THEN 'MON'
                                           WHEN 3 THEN 'TUE' WHEN 4 THEN 'WED'
                                           WHEN 5 THEN 'THU' WHEN 6 THEN 'FRI'
                                           WHEN 7 THEN 'SAT'
                                       END) > 0))
                ) THEN 'Y'
                ELSE 'N'
           END AS EDIT_FLAG
         , '999' AS SORT_KEY
         , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(50)) AS GROUP_ID
         , PVT.SUN, PVT.MON, PVT.TUE, PVT.WED, PVT.THU, PVT.FRI, PVT.SAT
         , CAL.YYYYMM, CAL.YYYYMMWW, CAL.WEEK_END_YN, CAL.MONTH_END_YN
         , 'N' AS NEW_FLAG
         , 'N' AS AS_YN
         , VD.VMI_STD_DIO AS VMI_STD_DIO
         , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(4000)) AS ATTR1
         , F.ROWID AS RID_
    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE F
    INNER JOIN CAL ON CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = F.YYYYMMDD
    LEFT JOIN PVT ON  PVT.DIVISION_ID = F.DIVISION_ID
                  AND PVT.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID
                  AND PVT.INCOTERMS   = F.INCOTERMS
    LEFT JOIN VD ON VD.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID
    /* MSP 항등 경로: 11키×일 유일 실측 — GROUP BY 생략 */
)
, M1 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, CASE WHEN X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '/' OR (X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) IS NULL) THEN '-' ELSE X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID END AS CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.OWNER, X.DEPT_ID, X.ITEM_TYPE, X.PROJECT_NAME, X.PROJECT_OWNER, X.BIZ_TYPE, X.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, X.COUNTRY_NAME, X.CITY_NAME, X.PROJECT_STATUS, X.ITEM_NAME, X.VMI_YN, X.CELL_PER_CAR, X.CELL_PER_MODULE, X.MODULE_PER_CAR, X.CREATE_DATE, X.LAST_UPDATED_BY, X.SENDER_NAME, X.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1, X.RID_ FROM MSP X)
, ATTR1S AS (
            SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
                 , NVL(A1,'')  || '^' || NVL(A2,'')  || '^' || NVL(A3,'')  || '^' || NVL(A4,'')  || '^' || NVL(A5,'')
                || '^' || NVL(A6,'')  || '^' || NVL(A7,'')  || '^' || NVL(A8,'')  || '^' || NVL(A9,'')  || '^' || NVL(A10,'')
                || '^' || NVL(A11,'') || '^' || NVL(A12,'') || '^' || NVL(A13,'') || '^' || NVL(A14,'') || '^' || NVL(A15,'')
                || '^' || NVL(A16,'') || '^' || NVL(A17,'') || '^' || NVL(A18,'') || '^' || 
 '^' || NVL(A23,'') || '^' || NVL(A24,'') || '^' || NVL(A25,'')
                || '^' || NVL(A26,'') || '^' || NVL(A27,'') || '^' || NVL(A28,'') || '^' || NVL(A29,'') || '^' || NVL(A30,'')
                || '^' || NVL(A31,'') AS ATTR1
            FROM (
                SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
                     , YYYYMMDD || '/'
                       || CASE
                              WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(ATTRIBUTE3,'0'),'^[0-9.]+$')
                               AND A.QTY >
                                   CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(ATTRIBUTE3,'0'),'^[0-9.]+$')
                                        THEN TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE3) ELSE 0 END
                                 + CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(ATTRIBUTE2,'0'),'^[0-9.]+$')
                                        THEN TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE2) ELSE 0 END
                              THEN 'P'
                              ELSE 'A'
                          END AS SYNC_YN
                     , 'A' || TO_CHAR(
                           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
                               PARTITION BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID,
                                            DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
                               ORDER BY YYYYMMDD
                           )) AS ROW_NUM
                FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
                WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = '40'
                  AND  A.MEASURE     = 'MKT_FCST'
                  AND  A.YYYYMMDD   BETWEEN '20261102' AND '20270430'
                  AND  ( A.ATTRIBUTE4 IS NOT NULL OR A.ATTRIBUTE5 IS NOT NULL )
                  AND  A.QTY <> 0
            ) A
            PIVOT (
                MAX(SYNC_YN) FOR ROW_NUM IN (
                    'A1'  A1,  'A2'  A2,  'A3'  A3,  'A4'  A4,  'A5'  A5,
                    'A6'  A6,  'A7'  A7,  'A8'  A8,  'A9'  A9,  'A10' A10,
                    'A11' A11, 'A12' A12, 'A13' A13, 'A14' A14, 'A15' A15,
                    'A16' A16, 'A17' A17, 'A18' A18, 'A19' A19, 'A20' A20,
                    'A21' A21, 'A22' A22, 'A23' A23, 'A24' A24, 'A25' A25,
                    'A26' A26, 'A27' A27, 'A28' A28, 'A29' A29, 'A30' A30,
                    'A31' A31
                )
            ))
, M2 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.OWNER, X.DEPT_ID, X.ITEM_TYPE, X.PROJECT_NAME, X.PROJECT_OWNER, X.BIZ_TYPE, X.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, X.COUNTRY_NAME, X.CITY_NAME, X.PROJECT_STATUS, X.ITEM_NAME, X.VMI_YN, X.CELL_PER_CAR, X.CELL_PER_MODULE, X.MODULE_PER_CAR, X.CREATE_DATE, X.LAST_UPDATED_BY, X.SENDER_NAME, X.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, S.ATTR1 AS ATTR1, X.RID_ FROM M1 X
            LEFT JOIN ATTR1S S
              ON (    X.DIVISION_ID  = S.DIVISION_ID
                  AND X.PROJECT_CODE = S.PROJECT_CODE
                  AND X.SCM_SITE_ID  = S.SCM_SITE_ID
                  AND X.ITEM_ID      = S.ITEM_ID
                  AND X.DOM_EXP      = S.DOM_EXP
                  AND X.FCST_TYPE    = S.FCST_TYPE
                  AND X.INCOTERMS    = S.INCOTERMS))
, M3R AS (SELECT NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'/') AS K1, NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'/') AS K2, NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'/') AS K3, NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'/') AS K4, NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'/') AS K5, NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'/') AS K6, NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'/') AS K7, NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'/') AS K8, NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'/') AS K9, NVL(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,'/') AS K10, MAX(X.AS_FROM_SITE) AS AFS_MX_ FROM M2 X WHERE X.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST' AND RTRIM(X.AS_FROM_SITE, ' -/') IS NOT NULL GROUP BY NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'/'), NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'/'), NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'/'), NVL(X
,'/'), NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'/'), NVL(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,'/') HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT X.AS_FROM_SITE) = 1)
, M3 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.OWNER, X.DEPT_ID, X.ITEM_TYPE, X.PROJECT_NAME, X.PROJECT_OWNER, X.BIZ_TYPE, X.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, X.COUNTRY_NAME, X.CITY_NAME, X.PROJECT_STATUS, X.ITEM_NAME, X.VMI_YN, X.CELL_PER_CAR, X.CELL_PER_MODULE, X.MODULE_PER_CAR, X.CREATE_DATE, X.LAST_UPDATED_BY, X.SENDER_NAME, X.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, CASE WHEN X.MEASURE <> 'MKT_FCST' AND RTRIM(X.AS_FROM_SITE, ' -/') IS NULL AND R.AFS_MX_ IS NOT NULL THEN R.AFS_MX_ ELSE X.AS_FROM_SITE END AS AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1, X.RID_ FROM M2 X LEFT JOIN M3R R ON (NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'/') = R.K1 AND NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'/') = R.K2 AND NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'/') = R.K3 AND NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'/') = R.K4 AND NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'/') = R.K5 AND NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'/') = R.K6 AND NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'/') = R.K7 AND NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'/') = R.K8 AND NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'/') = R.K9 AND NVL(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,'/') = R.K10))
, M4 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.OWNER, X.DEPT_ID, X.ITEM_TYPE, X.PROJECT_NAME, X.PROJECT_OWNER, X.BIZ_TYPE, X.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, X.COUNTRY_NAME, X.CITY_NAME, X.PROJECT_STATUS, X.ITEM_NAME, X.VMI_YN, X.CELL_PER_CAR, X.CELL_PER_MODULE, X.MODULE_PER_CAR, X.CREATE_DATE, X.LAST_UPDATED_BY, X.SENDER_NAME, X.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, CASE WHEN X.MEASURE NOT IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'USD', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW') AND (X.CURRENCY IS NULL OR TRIM(X.CURRENCY) IS NULL OR TRIM(X.CURRENCY) = '/') THEN ' ' ELSE X.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1, X.RID_ FROM M3 X)
, SRCA AS (SELECT X.*, X.DEPT_ID AS TEAM_ID, NVL(TO_CHAR(X.CREATE_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), ' ') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NVL(TO_CHAR(X.SUN),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.MON),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.TUE),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.WED),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.THU),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.FRI),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.SAT),'0') AS BUCKET FROM M4 X)
, LUW AS (SELECT X.RID_, MAX(X.CREATE_DATE) OVER (PARTITION BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_TYPE, OWNER, VMI_YN, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_CODE, PLANT_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, BIZ_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, PROJECT_OWNER, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, SORT_ORDER_NO) AS LU_DATE_, MAX(X.LAST_UPDATED_BY) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST ORDER BY X.CREATE_DATE NULLS FIRST) OVER (PARTITION BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_TYPE, OWNER, VMI_YN, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_CODE, PLANT_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, BIZ_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, PROJECT_OWNER, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, SORT_ORDER_NO) AS LU_BY_ FROM SRCA X WHERE ((X.MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FC
VISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.OWNER, X.DEPT_ID, X.ITEM_TYPE, X.PROJECT_NAME, X.PROJECT_OWNER, X.BIZ_TYPE, X.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, X.COUNTRY_NAME, X.CITY_NAME, X.PROJECT_STATUS, X.ITEM_NAME, X.VMI_YN, X.CELL_PER_CAR, X.CELL_PER_MODULE, X.MODULE_PER_CAR, CASE WHEN ((X.MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND X.QTY <> 0) OR X.MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP')) THEN R.LU_DATE_ ELSE X.CREATE_DATE END AS CREATE_DATE, CASE WHEN ((X.MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND X.QTY <> 0) OR X.MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP')) THEN R.LU_BY_ ELSE X.LAST_UPDATED_BY END AS LAST_UPDATED_BY, X.SENDER_NAME, X.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1, X.RID_, X.TEAM_ID, NVL(TO_CHAR(CASE WHEN ((X.MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND X.QTY <> 0) OR X.MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP')) THEN R.LU_DATE_ ELSE X.CREATE_DATE END, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), ' ') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE, X.BUCKET FROM SRCA X LEFT JOIN LUW R ON (X.RID_ = R.RID_))
, DISP AS (SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_TYPE, OWNER, VMI_YN, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_CODE, PLANT_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, BIZ_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, PROJECT_OWNER, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, SORT_ORDER_NO, YYYYMM, YYYYMMWW, WEEK_END_YN, MONTH_END_YN, BUCKET_KEY, SUM(X.QTY) AS QTY, COUNT(*) AS CNT_ FROM SRC0 X WHERE ((X.MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND X.QTY <> 0) OR X.MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP')) GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_TYPE, OWNER, VMI_YN, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_CODE, PLANT_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, BIZ_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, PROJECT_OWNER, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, SORT_ORDER_NO, YYYYMM, YYYYMMWW, WEEK_END_YN, MONTH_END_YN, BUCKET_KEY)

SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD , A.BASIS_YYYYMM , CASE WHEN SUBSTR(A.BASIS_START_DATE, -2) = '01' THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS FIRST_WEEK_FLAG , CASE WHEN SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(A.BASIS_START_DATE,'YYYYMMDD')+7,'YYYYMMDD'),1,6) = SUBSTR(A.BASIS_START_DATE,1,6) THEN 'N' ELSE 'Y' END AS END_MONTH_FLAG , COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM) AS MONTH_DAY_CNT , COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY A.ISO_YYYYWW || A.PARTIAL_WEEK_NAME) AS WEEK_DAY_CNT , A.ISO_WEEK FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A WHERE A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 ORDER BY A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD

END OF STMT
